ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e6644	Madam President. Dear Madam Minister of the Interior! Ladies and gentlemen! If we are talking about a crime explosion in the title of today's current hour, we are doing so quite consciously and for good reason. If we know the numbers of the security monitor and know what these numbers look like on the security monitor before they are reworked and manipulated in the Ministry of the Interior before presenting an official Ministry of the Interior statistics to the public, we must note that reality looks completely different from those that are presented by the Ministry of the Interior month after month to the public in a statistics. [[Admissibility at the FPÖ]] I have repeatedly pointed out in the past that, unfortunately, there is a statistical analysis of crime figures, which is handled as follows: If motor vehicles are stolen in a alley or in a quarter of a city or village – for example, 20 motor vehicles in a alley or in a quarter – and the perpetrator is not caught, then in today's statistics of the Ministry of the Interior, this leads to just taking one offense into the statistics. Although 20 stolen motor vehicles would represent 20 offences, this is only taken as a crime in the statistics! If the culprit should be caught as a result, one goes here and enters into Statistics 20 enlightened offences that are presented to the public. This is not only a very strange process, but also a false way of dealing with figures and statistics! [[Congratulations at the FPÖ.]] I say that is an unbearable method where one's own Austrian population – in the truest sense of the word! - trying to sell for stupid. I will therefore mention the numbers of the security monitor. We have the figures from November 2008 to January 2009 compared to November 2007 to 2008 in order to know the comparison figures. During this period, there has been an incredible increase of 22 percent in housing collapses in Vienna alone. That's about 2,760 burglaries only in Vienna! That is to say, there were 500 collapses more than in the period 2007 to 2008. The number of house collapses has even increased by 100 percent in Vienna, from 290 cuts in 2007/2008 to 770 in November 2008 to January 2009. In Vienna, according to executive officials, there is a peak of between 100 and 150 cases every day when attempts are being made and collapses in apartments, houses, offices and business premises. He therefore always commutes between 100 and 150 attempts at burglary per day in Vienna. Let's just take as an example the 30th. January 2009! On that day there were 1 542 offences in Vienna, 233 of them were violent offences, 974 property crimes, 218 vehicle offences, where there is not a false parking, but car burglaries and car thefts. – That alone shows how dramatic the development is. In Carinthia, there is an increase in crime by 5 percent, including these figures. The number of burglaries in Carinthia rose by 22 percent from 676 to 830. There is also an increase in Salzburg. So this is not an exclusively eastern phenomenon, that we only increase dramatic crime in Vienna would have! There was also an increase in overall crime by 4 percent in Salzburg. In Salzburg, the number of invasions increased by 19 percent from 1 040 to 1 260. By contrasting the figures and data of total Austria in the security monitor with the published statistics of the Ministry of the Interior, you can only be surprised. In 2009, a total of 135 300 offences were reported on the security monitor. The top riders are burglary, theft, robbery and property damage. In total Austria, there is an increase of 14 percent, which is by 16 720 offences more than in the period 2007 to 2008. At that time it was still 118,580 offences. This shows that crime is increasing dramatically and is beautifully colored by this red-black federal government, by Mrs. Fekter's Minister of the Interior, false figures are published, and one tries to simply put problems that are present under the table. That's not responsible, Mrs. Interior Minister! [[Congratulations at the FPÖ.]] In fact, executive officials state very openly that since the Schengen border opening, crime has increased dramatically in total Austria, especially but in eastern Austria – in some sub-areas by 40 percent, in the area of house collapses by 61 percent. I expressly emphasize: only in the field of car theft, as the car breaks are not statistically recorded. It shows how sad reality in this area unfortunately looks to God. Point Government Template: Additional Protocol to the Geneva Agreements of 12. August 1949 on the adoption of an additional trademark (Protocol III) (50 d.a.) (in accordance with § 28a GOG no committee preliminary advice)
ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e16290	We're on the 9th. Agenda item. The preliminary consultation in a committee was suspended in accordance with Article 28a of the Rules of Procedure. ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e16317	Mr President. (Parliament adopted the legislative resolution) This item is an additional protocol to the Geneva Agreement on the adoption of an additional protective mark (Protocol III), i.e. the use of the red crystal. In addition to the well-known protective mark of the red cross or the red hemisphere, the red crystal is introduced here in addition as a protective or marking mark. What rules this Additional Protocol? – It is regulated that the red crystal is additionally introduced and recognised as a protection and marking. [[Abg. Grosz: How does that look?]] - What does that look like? The red crystal is visually represented as a red frame in the form of a square on top of the white ground, Mr Grosz, I believe that this is understandable and is introduced for those countries that do not want to accept the red cross or the red half moon. What are the benefits of the Additional Protocol for Austria? – In principle, it does not change the use of the red cross as a protection or marking by the health services of the Austrian Federal Army and the Austrian Red Cross. However, this Protocol grants the additional authorisation to use the red crystal temporarily under certain conditions, such as if this increases protection or facilitates work. Austrian participation in missions under the umbrella of the United Nations could also benefit from an additional possibility of protection through the red crystal. 35 states have joined and ratified this. And I think we should do that in Austria too. Finally, ladies and gentlemen, I would also like to take this opportunity to say a sincere thank you to all the employees of the Red Cross, who are now returning to these snow masses and avalanches, namely all volunteer officials, but also to the professional and civil servants working around the clock for the entire population.

ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e16375	Mr President. Minister! High house! The 2005 Additional Protocol III to the Geneva Agreements is intended to introduce an additional neutral sign, the red crystal. Since the protective signs of the Geneva agreements, the red cross and the red half moon, are often understood as religious signs, this sign is the only right alternative. This will enable national societies that do not want to accept the red cross or the red hemisphere or both signs to join the international Red Cross and Red Hemisphere movement. It will be important that the protective mark of red crystal finds international recognition, thus increasing protection for organisations and facilitating work. Among these aspects, the introduction of the protective mark of red crystal is only to be welcomed.

That is to say, these people migrate to us through a secure third country, and their procedures are not what we are going to do, but are to deal with other countries. At present, we have many Dublin cases in the Federal Service, and we also need to upgrade infrastructure to bring them more quickly to the country where their procedures are to be carried out. ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e25094	Mr President. High house! Financial penalties are not a cavalier's crime, no matter in any European country. [[Abg. Weinzinger: Financial wrongdoing, but not financial punishment!]] It is therefore necessary to have an effective EU financial penalty law. It is about implementing the EU Framework Decision on the mutual recognition of fines and fines in administrative financial penalties. The fines and fines imposed on infringements of tax and customs legislation shall be recorded. This is a system for the enforcement of fines and fines imposed by courts of other Member States in the case of decisions of the financial and customs authorities, as well as criminal proceedings, in the case of penalties imposed by courts of other Member States, and for the enforcement of decisions of Austrian financial criminal authorities in another EU Member State, so that they can recognise and bring together, without further formalities, the corresponding penalties provided for in their country of the offences. In the framework decision, there are some facts, such as smuggling of goods or counterfeiting and piracy of products. It's interesting – and you should know that too: Since 2005, nearly 8,000 such applications have been filed for enforcement. This Framework Decision has also been implemented in the field of justice. The proceeds from these executions remain in the respective country of enforcement. The costs shall also be borne, with a balanced result being assumed. This is a sensible law, and I am seeking approval.

(Parliament adopted the legislative resolution) In fact, I would just like to join with a practical example where Mr Mayer has stopped. It is precisely for this reason that it is necessary and important that this Framework Decision be implemented now – it has already been due since 2005. I would also like to point out that twelve countries of the European Union have already implemented this Framework Decision. In particular, I note that the young EU countries, such as Latvia, Lithuania, Slovenia – to name just a few – have already implemented this and are still lagging behind the older EU countries. I think it is important and necessary that we quickly reach a result throughout the European Union in order to be able to close this gap. - Thank you.

ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e25767	Madam President. Mr State Secretary! (Parliament adopted the legislative resolution) So I cannot agree with the opinion of Mr Zanger of the FPÖ: In any case, when a reform commission is convened, it is right that these professionals are represented, who know how matter is obtained, and who can also assess what measures to be taken to restore the second pillar of pension provision to that stability and confidence that it needs – and to which we confess. The fact that, in the political sphere, other interest groups will, of course, be involved in this process – as well as the association of protection – is of course a matter of course for me. As regards the legislation to be adopted today, it should be noted that the national implementation of the EU Directive, which regulates supervisory rights for financial undertakings in the acquisition of other financial undertakings, corresponds to the previous administrative practice in Austria. I would particularly like to stress that I believe that this law is of enormous quality and that it is to be thanked in particular to the officials of the Ministry of Finance, who cannot at the moment complain about too little work. Now on an aspect which has not yet been mentioned and which concerns a change in the Stock Exchange Act. In this context, I recall the certificates issued by Meinl European Land, where it turned out – one of the major weaknesses – that certificates listed are not subject to the same transparency rules as shares. – We are now repairing this defect with this novel, so that those consequences, as they have occurred in the case of the MEL to the detriment of investors, will be excluded for the investor in the future. Furthermore, to emphasize this is essential to me, because a number of discussions have also taken place today, in the current legislative innovations, we are finally transferring a committee from 2007 on the occasion of a change in the rules of commerce into a legal form, so that the credit economy, if it distributes insurance, will once again have sufficient legal clarity and legal certainty. The address of the insurance brokers and their interest groups must be addressed and repeatedly underlined that the current market situation and the status quo in the competition do not experience any changes, but only the corresponding legal implementation that was already planned at the time. On one last point there is a corresponding note: We will be discussing today a motion for a resolution which we want to re-enter the admission of foreign currency credits for private individuals in the sense of the bank's duty of care, but above all from the point of view of customer protection. ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e26995	Next, Mr Heinzl. 3 minutes. - Please.
ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e27017	Madam President. Thank you, Mrs. Minister! High house! The increase in road safety is one of the central concerns of all of us, as I believe, and our common objective is that in the future there should be no serious injury and no more deaths on Austria's roads. Road safety is indeed a complex problem. We all know that the best electronic helpers or airbags do not help and do not provide sufficient protection at over-speed, shaving and pushing. The result is mostly dead and seriously injured. Ladies and gentlemen of the FPÖ, Mr Vock, I can already tell you that the regulations of the road transport system are really not arbitrary, but they are for their own protection – also for your protection and for the protection of others. [[Congratulations with the SPÖ.]] Ladies and gentlemen, many methods of traffic monitoring that have been practised for years are now finally regulated precisely by law, as mentioned by the Section Control, the use of radar, so-called red light cameras and the filming of traffic sinners from police cars. For the first time in Austria there is now also the possibility of radar measurements from the front. This will facilitate cross-border law enforcement. High house! Of course, in determining all these monitoring methods legally – this is very important! – data protection does not go unnoticed. The data obtained by traffic monitoring are highly sensitive. It is our task to protect the citizens from total surveillance. We certainly do not want to have any big brother ratios in Austria! [[Congratulations with the SPÖ.]] Therefore, strict data protection provisions are laid down within the framework of the legislation on road transport. For example, pictures of storage cameras may only live the passenger must be identified, data may only be used for administrative criminal proceedings. If the data are not used for law enforcement, they must be deleted immediately. Data used must also be deleted after use. The Transport Committee, ladies and gentlemen, has decided to establish a committee. Video traffic monitoring will only occur in particularly dangerous sections of the road, such as tunnels, construction sites, where there are many traffic accidents, and where environmental limits are exceeded. The Committee also agreed that a success report on the use of Section Control will be submitted to the National Council in two years. High house! Ladies and gentlemen! Today's amendment to the road transport regulations will make an important contribution to road safety, as I believe, and road safety is at our heart. - Thank you very much.

Of course, this has to be countered. However, it should meet not only the Austrians, but all those who drive on Austria's roads.

Many of these serious accidents are just caused by shavers, throttlers and all those who do not adhere to the StVO – not only in our homeland Carinthia. I think it is particularly important that the present STO-novelle also takes into account the data protection concerns expressed by many citizens. This is ensured, for example, by ensuring that very restrictive data-use and erasure schemes are applied. For the consent of us Social Democrats to this novel, it is essential that the personal data obtained from detected infringement cases should only be used for administrative penalties immediately thereafter – and for nothing else. This prevents any abuse of personal data. In order for Austria's media landscape to be beneficial in the future from that of the United Kingdom or Germany, all technical measures are also taken to ensure that uninformed participants who are visible in the photographs are immediately erased and data from third parties who have not committed administrative violation are immediately deleted. This system is also intended to protect the privacy of the persons involved. On the whole, it can be said that this 22. Road traffic regulation Novelle Austria's roads will make it safer and, hopefully, many sufferings that have occurred so far remain in the future – and this will be done with the highest data protection requirements. Thank you.

ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e28109	The next speaker is Mr Singer. 2 minutes of voluntary time limit. - Please.
ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e28130	Madam President. Thank you, Mrs. Federal Minister! (Parliament adopted the legislative resolution) The present amendment to the Road Traffic Regulations and the Road Traffic Act presents essential measures for decision-making, which increase the control possibilities of transport and thus the safety of our roads. Ladies and gentlemen, around 46 percent of all accidents on Austria's motorways were caused by foreign citizens. Considering that speed violations are the cause of a third of the fatal accidents, the use of front photography is more than justified. In this way, the law enforcement of German drivers can be brought about, and the current inequality of domestic steering controls can be eliminated. The high proportion of speed crossings as a cause of fatal accidents also justify sectional speed monitoring, which is used if it is urgently necessary to increase road safety and thus to protect the population or the environment, i.e. if a particular hazard situation is encountered. Essentially to the protection of pedestrians and thus also of children, the automation-supported identification of a red light sign contributes to the lack of respect. In general, I believe that automation support also means discharge of human resources in monitoring, thereby contributing to strengthening security on Austrian roads as a whole. Supporting road safety is also the possibility of live traffic monitoring: Disorders in the traffic flow such as congestion, smoothness, snow road or fog formation can be detected quickly and it can react quickly. That one. – In cooperation with the media, this also results in a further service for citizens. I also take a positive view of the provisions concerning data protection: Data may only be used for the purposes of administrative procedures, all others must be identified or deleted. Ladies and gentlemen, I am convinced that the proposed changes to the road transport innovations will contribute to the safety of Austrian roads and thus serve to protect our people. Thank you.

Point Report of the Transport Committee on the Government Template (34 e.g._Other Organiser Federal Act amending the Shipping Act (73 d.b.)
ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e28334	We arrive at the 24th. Agenda item. There was no oral reporting. First of all, Mrs Mag. Lohfeyer. 2 minutes of voluntary time limit. - Please.
ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e28361	Madam President. Mrs. Federal Minister! High house! This revision of the Shipping Act is necessary because there is an obligation to transpose a 2006 EU directive on technical requirements for inland waterways into national law. The aim of this innovation is to create the legal conditions for recasting the shipping technology regulation – the draft is currently under review. Harmonization of EU rules and the rules of the Central Commission for Rhine-Westphalia should bring about the same technical rules for the entire coherent network of waterways across the European continent. Vehicles therefore no longer have to be equipped with several certificates of approval, a national or foreign licence is entitled to travel on Austrian waters. In return, a check of conformity with the technical requirements is possible at any time for each competent authority within the framework of the State control. Overall, it must be said that these and other innovations bring not only greater legal certainty for all involved in waterway transport, but also fairer competitive conditions and simplification for users. – For this reason, this innovation is to be welcomed.

Point Government template: Agreement between the Republic of Austria and the Socialist Republic of Vietnam on the mutual recognition of equality in higher education (51 d.b.) (in accordance with § 28a GOG no committee preliminary advice)
ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e30193	We're coming to the 29th. The item on the agenda and the debate. Mr Mayer is speaking on behalf of the Committee on the Environment, Public Health and Consumer - Please.
ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e30217	Mr. Minister! Ladies and gentlemen! In my short speech, I can also act as rapporteur, in some way as rapporteur, because this presentation did not happen to the Committee. It is about the presentation that the issue of recognition in higher education between the Republic of Austria and the Socialist Republic of Vietnam is becoming more and more frequent because of the strong rise in mutual cultural relations. The universities of both Contracting States need a secure basis for recognition procedures. The Agreement lays down the conditions under which the study and examination services at the universities of the two Contracting States are mutually recognised, give diplomas a right to further studies and to conduct academic degrees. The agreement was formulated on the basis of contacts between the ministers of the Contracting States responsible for higher education, as well as consultations between Austrian and Vietnamese experts. The representative bodies of the universities and the higher education sector have been informed of each step and have agreed to the procedure. The aim of the agreement is to establish equality in higher education, i.e. mutual recognition of diplomas, examinations and academic degrees, in certain aspects. It is intended to relieve universities of the assessment of the academic partial benefit in individual cases, without, however, automatically recognizing full studies or affecting decisions regarding professional activities. The agreement allows the individual Austrian higher education institutions to introduce the question of the quality of the Vietnamese training in question as a criterion in the decisions. This takes sufficient account of the need for quality assurance of higher education studies. I ask you to vote on this. - Thank you.
ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e30254	We shall come to the vote in accordance with Rule 65 of the Rules of Procedure. The subject-matter is the approval of the State Treaty in force: Agreements with the Socialist Republic of Vietnam on the mutual recognition of equality in higher education in 51 of the Supplements. In the event of approval, I would ask for an appropriate sign. – That is unanimously decided. [[23:42:26]] 30. Point Report by the Constitutional Committee on Amendment 425/A by Mr Karlheinz Kopf, Dr. Josef Cap, colleagues on a Federal Act amending the European Elections Act and the European Elections Act (84 d.b.)
ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e30299	We're coming to 30. The item on the agenda and the debate. Mr Magistrate is speaking on behalf of the Group of the European People's Party (Christian Democrats). Stefan. - Please.
ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e30323	Mr President. Ladies and gentlemen! It is about the introduction of the election of letters in the European electoral order. We liberals have already spoken against this in the introduction of the letter election, and I am well aware of that. Because it turned out that we are not alone here, but that leading constitutional lawyers in Austria represent our very opinion, one hundred percent, for example Heinz Mayer or Karl Weber of the University of Innsbruck, who use our arguments exactly. This includes the fact that the general right to vote is not guaranteed here on the one hand. Because we know from the last National Council election that some postcards have not arrived. There are, of course, no accurate statistics on this, but after many complaints have already been made to us, I assume that it must have been at least hundreds. All those persons are thus excluded from their right to vote, so the general right to vote is not guaranteed here. The right of free elections is not guaranteed either. Because we know very well that now, of course, the influence is immediately open at the vote door and gate. We have already seen that in Carinthia, for example, since the country elections are already possible the choice of letters, letters are collected with authority. We can then imagine about how they are filled in. We can imagine how families may be tuned, how perhaps they are voted in clubs or in larger family associations. In any case, it is no longer guaranteed what was always a very important principle: that the election must take place in such a way that no one else can take an insight into the vote. That's why you have to go into the constituency - we know that very well - and this is no longer guaranteed here. So a very essential principle of election is not possible. [[Congratulations at the FPÖ.]] In addition, the right to vote is limited as follows: So far, it has always been necessary for one to give his voice as unimpeded as possible, so there was the first high-end account only after all the constituencies were closed. Now I have the opportunity – and this will be simplified with this choice of letters – to give my voice after the high billing. After the new draft, eight days after the election day, the vote must be taken by the District Election Authority. I confirm with a signature that the vote has taken place before, but we know very well that this is not the case. This is why the “Schümmelpicker” is here Open door and gate and do not guarantee the undesired choice, so this principle does not comply with either. The secret election is neither guaranteed. We know exactly what on the postway can be lost again towards the constituency, i.e. district election authority, and that this can be opened. It's on the envelope who gives the voice, and there's a signature on it. Those who are curious can see who gave what voice. Therefore, compliance with this principle is not guaranteed. It is really amazing that this is equally seen by the constitutional lawyers. Today we have even experienced that, interestingly, the Austrian high school has joined this criticism. The former President of the Constitutional Court of Justice of Korinek has also made a very devastating judgment here. The next stage will then be the e-voting, even that should already be in front of the door. We're so modern. But we know very well that there have already been major problems in such attempts, for example in England and Finland. Our experts tell us, however, that will be safe in Austria. However, the list of safe computer systems, which were then hacked and manipulated, is very large, and it is obvious that there is no really safe system here either. By the way, I would like to know who will then ensure that the choice is safe, unimpeded and not manipulated. It may only be possible to confirm this to very few computer specialists, if at all, but otherwise that is not the case. So everything here is extremely problematic. We are therefore proposing an amendment which has already been distributed. Therefore, the election of the letter should only be allowed for voters from abroad, while ensuring that the vote has taken place before the election day. “verified” means that there is already a corresponding confirmation. However, the choice of letters in the country is deleted without replacement. I can only ask you: Take our concerns seriously, and do not sacrifice the principles of our right to vote for the seemingly modern choice of letter!

Point First reading: Parliament's motion for a resolution (Doc. Albert Steinhauser, colleagues in the Federal Law amending the Citizenship Act 1985 (Citizen's Change Act 2008) (150/A)
ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e32170	We'll get to 34. Agenda item. Mr Mag. Steinhauser, you're talking.
ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e32194	Ladies and gentlemen! 64 years after the end of the Nazi terror rule, Austria is still struggling with the shadows of the past. There are many examples of this. Over 30 years in Austria, no war criminals have been sentenced. Mr. Aschner – formerly Ašner – a suspected war criminal in Croatia is allowed to walk in Carinthia. We find it difficult to rehabilitate the Nazi victims, we are hard to care for Jewish cemeteries, and we are also struggling with full rehabilitation in citizenship. While it is necessary to admit that 1993 has improved the legal situation for NS persecuted persons who have lost citizenship, it is possible to obtain easier citizenship, but a full restoration, a full restitution has not been given. This was also noted by the Historical Commission in the mid-1990s. It said at the time that our legal situation still assumes that the NSAs have lost citizenship. It is completely overlooked that the loss of citizenship has never been intentioned by the NSAs, but they have been driven out, persecuted and, if they have adopted a new citizenship, they have been taken for protection. The fact is that this has done about a third of all displaced people. What was the result? – Although the citizenship has been abolished, the Second Republic, after 1945, has also brought into force those laws which have created losses if a new citizenship has been adopted. That is to say, there has been a second wave of citizenship. The NSAs have been expelled by the Second Republic of the Jure and de facto. This situation is still open, and it is precisely what our proposal for a law is aimed at: That this lack of restitution will be eliminated. We have already made this request in the last legislative period, and I was surprised how quickly I received international feedback. I have received e-mails from the United States, from Singapore, where people who have read this have said, great that there is this initiative, because that is still open and it is necessary. We must create a situation where it is clear that these people have never lost their citizenship. These were Austrians who wanted to remain citizens. Therefore, a decision should be adopted as easily as possible if people wish them to be Austrian citizens. Secondly, this would have a great advantage for the descendants of these people, because we know that in Austria, the acquisition of citizenship to the parents is linked, and according to the current legal situation, it is not possible for the descendants of the NS persecuted to regain Austrian citizenship if the parent generation was excreted, no longer wanted to be re-citizened and has now passed away. This law would satisfy the Historic Commission. If our initiative were to be implemented, then the Republic would at least have done its culprit with regard to citizenship. Thank you.

ParlaMint-AT_2009-02-26-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00014_d8e33563	Mr President. High house! We know that we are committed to saving 13 percent of greenhouse gas emissions in 2008 to 2012 compared to 1990 in Kyoto. By 2007, however, we were at a plus of 11 percent. And we also know that CO2 emissions, CO2 reduction is directly related to energy consumption or the reduction of energy consumption. Therefore, in the sense of climate protection, it is also a duty to deal with energy carefully, to deal efficiently with energy, also in order to protect resources. The Eighth Environmental Control Report also proposes an increase in energy efficiency. We Greens have proposed a comprehensive climate change package and today we have introduced an essential element of energy efficiency. One of the main emitters of greenhouse gas emissions in addition to transport is industry. We therefore provide for the inclusion of energy efficiency as a criterion in plant law. Operating plants should only be authorised if the energy used there is ensured to be used efficiently if an energy efficiency concept is presented for approval, which contains information on the energy consumption in the plant, on the use of the energy that is involved in the installation, and also a description of those measures that are set up to achieve the maximum possible efficiency of the plant. These proposals go beyond the existing arrangements. The main objective of the existing schemes is to reduce emissions. However, energy saving potentials are also in other areas of plants. This project therefore represents an improvement in the direction of greater energy efficiency. Energy efficiency is to be introduced as an approval criterion into the operating plant law so that the authority can make appropriate decisions to be able to provide appropriate conditions in order to ensure the energy efficiency of industrial plants. Thank you.
